- Headings: SCWE-088: Improper Decimal Normalization in Price-Based Calculations; Relationships[¶](https://scs.owasp.org/SCWE/SCSVS-ORACLE/SCWE-088/#relationships "Permanent link"); Description[¶](https://scs.owasp.org/SCWE/SCSVS-ORACLE/SCWE-088/#description "Permanent link"); Impact[¶](https://scs.owasp.org/SCWE/SCSVS-ORACLE/SCWE-088/#impact "Permanent link"); Remediation[¶](https://scs.owasp.org/SCWE/SCSVS-ORACLE/SCWE-088/#remediation "Permanent link"); Examples[¶](https://scs.owasp.org/SCWE/SCSVS-ORACLE/SCWE-088/#examples "Permanent link"); Realistic Exploit Example[¶](https://scs.owasp.org/SCWE/SCSVS-ORACLE/SCWE-088/#realistic-exploit-example "Permanent link"); `[](https://scs.owasp.org/SCWE/SCSVS-ORACLE/SCWE-088/#__codelineno-2-1)Registrar fee = 0.01 ETH = 1e16 wei [](https://scs.owasp.org/SCWE/SCSVS-ORACLE/SCWE-088/#__codelineno-2-2)[](https://scs.owasp.org/SCWE/SCSVS-ORACLE/SCWE-088/#__codelineno-2-3)ETH/USD price = 3000 * 1e8 = 300000000000 [](https://scs.owasp.org/SCWE/SCSVS-ORACLE/SCWE-088/#__codelineno-2-4)[](https://scs.owasp.org/SCWE/SCSVS-ORACLE/SCWE-088/#__codelineno-2-5)USDC/USD price = 1 * 1e8 = 100000000 [](https://scs.owasp.org/SCWE/SCSVS-ORACLE/SCWE-088/#__codelineno-2-6)[](https://scs.owasp.org/SCWE/SCSVS-ORACLE/SCWE-088/#__codelineno-2-7)USDC has 6 decimals [](https://scs.owasp.org/SCWE/SCSVS-ORACLE/SCWE-088/#__codelineno-2-8)[](https://scs.owasp.org/SCWE/SCSVS-ORACLE/SCWE-088/#__codelineno-2-9)Faulty Calculation: [](https://scs.owasp.org/SCWE/SCSVS-ORACLE/SCWE-088/#__codelineno-2-10)[](https://scs.owasp.org/SCWE/SCSVS-ORACLE/SCWE-088/#__codelineno-2-11)result = (1e16 * 300000000000) / 100000000 = 3e19 [](https://scs.owasp.org/SCWE/SCSVS-ORACLE/SCWE-088/#__codelineno-2-12)return result / 1e6 = 3e13 (30 trillion base units = 30 million USDC) [](https://scs.owasp.org/SCWE/SCSVS-ORACLE/SCWE-088/#__codelineno-2-13)Expected: [](https://scs.owasp.org/SCWE/SCSVS-ORACLE/SCWE-088/#__codelineno-2-14)[](https://scs.owasp.org/SCWE/SCSVS-ORACLE/SCWE-088/#__codelineno-2-15)0.01 ETH * $3,000 = $30 [](https://scs.owasp.org/SCWE/SCSVS-ORACLE/SCWE-088/#__codelineno-2-16)→ 30 * 1e6 = 30,000,000 USDC units [](https://scs.owasp.org/SCWE/SCSVS-ORACLE/SCWE-088/#__codelineno-2-17)[](https://scs.owasp.org/SCWE/SCSVS-ORACLE/SCWE-088/#__codelineno-2-18)Overcharge: [](https://scs.owasp.org/SCWE/SCSVS-ORACLE/SCWE-088/#__codelineno-2-19)[](https://scs.owasp.org/SCWE/SCSVS-ORACLE/SCWE-088/#__codelineno-2-20)Actual charged: 30,000,000 USDC [](https://scs.owasp.org/SCWE/SCSVS-ORACLE/SCWE-088/#__codelineno-2-21)Expected: 30 USDC [](https://scs.owasp.org/SCWE/SCSVS-ORACLE/SCWE-088/#__codelineno-2-22)Overcharge: 999,900x`
